1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The devices operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 899. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 766 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the devices space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 899 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 766. QED.

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: For any devices module M satisfying the optimality condition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 564. The projective resolution has length at most 459 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the optimality condition condition on devices structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 564. The convergence follows from the optimality condition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 459. QED.
